    def test_check_does_not_write_the_artifact():
        """`--check` must be read-only: a verify mode that regenerates cannot fail.
    
        ⭐ THIS IS THE TEST THAT DISCRIMINATES AGAINST THE DEFECT BEING REPLACED. The old behaviour
        would REWRITE the perturbed file and return 0. Return code alone does not separate the two --
        a regenerating check returns 0 because it just made the file match. Only the bytes do.
        """
        original = _original_bytes()
        try:
            doc = json.loads(original.decode("utf-8"))
>           doc["analyses"]["B_outcome_by_partner"]["disease_specific_death"]["taf15_arm"]["events"] = 1
            ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

research/manuscripts/tests/test_emc_fusion_partner_pooling_check.py:171: KeyError
_________ test_the_prose_asserts_the_containment_the_artifact_computes _________

flat = '--- id: DOC-EMC-FUSION-PARTNER-STRATIFICATION title: "Fusion-variant stratification in EMC (EWSR1::NR4A3 vs TAF15::NR...perseded, retained: "below the ≈27 % the field routinely quotes from a single referral-centre series" — Appendix A30. '
art = {'_schema': 'emc-fusion-partner-pooling/1', '_generated_by': 'research/manuscripts/emc_fusion_partner_pooling.py', '_g... in its COHORTS table. Change a count in the script and regenerate; never edit a number here (CLAUDE.md rule 1).', ...}

    def test_the_prose_asserts_the_containment_the_artifact_computes(flat, art):
        """★★ THE RELATION IS DERIVED, SO THE PROSE MUST FOLLOW IT — in both directions.
    
        `analyses.C_partner_prevalence.external_reported_share.falls_inside_pooled_interval` is computed
        from the pooled Wilson interval at generation time. If a count changes and that interval moves
        off the cited ≈20 %, that field flips to False and THIS test goes red — which is what makes the
        paper's central round-7 finding ("the pooling is consistent with its own cited literature, so the
        correction framing does not survive") a bound claim rather than a sentence somebody wrote once.
    
        ⛔ WITHOUT THIS, THE OPERANDS WERE BOUND AND THE CLAIM JOINING THEM WAS NOT. Binding 20 and
        12.9–25.0 separately says both numbers are right and says nothing about the sentence asserting
        one contains the other — which is the sentence carrying the finding. A claim is a QUANTITY and a
        RELATION and this guard set was built on the quantity half (paper-hardening §8b). Found by a
        blind regression seat, 2026-08-27, against a commit message that had claimed this was already
        implemented.
    
        ⛔ Both directions are asserted, because "the right word is present" and "no wrong word is
        present" fail differently, and over-anchoring one silently makes the check vacuous.
        """
        ext = art["analyses"]["C_partner_prevalence"]["external_reported_share"]
        inside = ext["falls_inside_pooled_interval"]
        lo, hi = ext["pooled_ci95_for_comparison"]
        assert (lo <= ext["percent_approx"] <= hi) is inside, (
            "the artifact's own derived containment disagrees with its own interval — regenerate it")
    
        if not inside:
            # The interval has moved off the cited share. The paper's round-7 finding no longer holds
            # and the prose must not still assert it. This branch is deliberately strict: it fails
            # until a human has re-read the section, which is the correct outcome for a finding that
            # has just been falsified by its own data.
            still = [w for w, p in _CONTAINMENT_REQUIRED if re.search(p, flat)]
            assert not still, (
                f"the artifact now derives falls_inside_pooled_interval=False over {lo}-{hi}, but the "
                f"prose still asserts containment at {still}. ⛔ The paper's finding changed — read the "
                "section, do not re-anchor the regex.")
            return
    
        missing = [w for w, p in _CONTAINMENT_REQUIRED if not re.search(p, flat)]
>       assert not missing, (
            f"the containment claim is absent at {missing}, while the artifact derives "
            f"falls_inside_pooled_interval=True over {lo}-{hi}. ⛔ CHECK THE MEANING BEFORE THE REGEX: "
            "re-anchor only if the sentence says the same thing in different words. If the sentence now "
            "says something else, the paper's finding changed and that is the thing to look at.")
E       AssertionError: the containment claim is absent at ['Abstract', '§1.3 what this synthesis adds', '§3.5', '§5 claims bullet'], while the artifact derives falls_inside_pooled_interval=True over 12.9-25.0. ⛔ CHECK THE MEANING BEFORE THE REGEX: re-anchor only if the sentence says the same thing in different words. If the sentence now says something else, the paper's finding changed and that is the thing to look at.
E       assert not ['Abstract', '§1.3 what this synthesis adds', '§3.5', '§5 claims bullet']

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("what,pattern,value,fold", BINDINGS, ids=[b[0] for b in BINDINGS])
    def test_every_site_that_states_the_quantity_states_the_artifacts_value(what, pattern, value, fold,
                                                                           flat, art):
        """⛔⛔ EVERY SITE, NOT WHETHER THE VALUE APPEARS SOMEWHERE.
    
        `re.findall` returns one entry per site; a corruption at ONE of several sites therefore fails
        here, which an `in` test cannot see. That distinction is the whole reason this file is shaped
        the way it is — see the module docstring.
    
        ⚠ AT LEAST ONE MATCH IS REQUIRED. A pattern that has stopped matching is a guard that has
        silently stopped guarding, so a reworded sentence goes RED rather than quiet. ⛔ And when it
        does: check the MEANING first. Re-anchor only if the sentence says the same thing in different
        words; if the claim itself changed, the fix is in the prose or the artifact, not in the regex.
        """
        expected = value(art)
        found = re.findall(pattern, flat)
>       assert found, (
            f"nothing in the manuscript matches the construction that states {what} (/{pattern}/). "
            "Either the sentence was reworded — check the MEANING before the regex — or the claim was "
            "dropped.")

n = 27

    def _word(n):
        """A count that reaches the page as a WORD is still a count, and binding it as one is the point.
    
        ⛔ RAISES ON AN UNMAPPED VALUE rather than falling back to digits: the failure this file exists
        for is a number changing, and a helper that quietly renders `four` as `4` when the artifact
        moves from three to four would make exactly that change invisible.
        """
        n = int(n)
        if n not in _WORDS:

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("what,pattern,value,fold", BINDINGS, ids=[b[0] for b in BINDINGS])
    def test_every_site_that_states_the_quantity_states_the_artifacts_value(what, pattern, value, fold,
                                                                           flat, art):
        """⛔⛔ EVERY SITE, NOT WHETHER THE VALUE APPEARS SOMEWHERE.
    
        `re.findall` returns one entry per site; a corruption at ONE of several sites therefore fails
        here, which an `in` test cannot see. That distinction is the whole reason this file is shaped
        the way it is — see the module docstring.
    
        ⚠ AT LEAST ONE MATCH IS REQUIRED. A pattern that has stopped matching is a guard that has
        silently stopped guarding, so a reworded sentence goes RED rather than quiet. ⛔ And when it
        does: check the MEANING first. Re-anchor only if the sentence says the same thing in different
        words; if the claim itself changed, the fix is in the prose or the artifact, not in the regex.
        """
        expected = value(art)
        found = re.findall(pattern, flat)
        assert found, (
            f"nothing in the manuscript matches the construction that states {what} (/{pattern}/). "
            "Either the sentence was reworded — check the MEANING before the regex — or the claim was "
            "dropped.")
        def same(got):
            if not fold:
                return got == expected
            if isinstance(expected, tuple):
                return tuple(g.casefold() for g in got) == tuple(e.casefold() for e in expected)
            return got.casefold() == expected.casefold()
    
        wrong = [f for f in found if not same(f)]
>       assert not wrong, (
            f"{what}: the artifact gives {expected!r} and the manuscript states {wrong!r} at "
            f"{len(wrong)} of its {len(found)} site(s). The artifact is generated from the counts in "
            "`emc_fusion_partner_pooling.py`; fix the prose, or fix the generator and regenerate — "
            "never paste the prose's value into this test.")
E       AssertionError: §3.3's count of the cohorts publishing outcome event counts: the artifact gives 'one' and the manuscript states ['Two'] at 1 of its 1 site(s). The artifact is generated from the counts in `emc_fusion_partner_pooling.py`; fix the prose, or fix the generator and regenerate — never paste the prose's value into this test.
E       assert not ['Two']

a = {'_schema': 'emc-fusion-partner-pooling/1', '_generated_by': 'research/manuscripts/emc_fusion_partner_pooling.py', '_g... in its COHORTS table. Change a count in the script and regenerate; never edit a number here (CLAUDE.md rule 1).', ...}

    def _sizes(a):
        """All four DERIVED over the cohort records rather than remembered.
    
        "Every TAF15 arm carrying an actual outcome" is every cohort with a TAF15 stratum that has a
        denominator — the two response arms and the two outcome arms — so the range is over exactly
        those and widens by itself if a fifth is added.
        """
        largest = max(c["n_assessable"] for c in a["cohorts"] if "n_assessable" in c)
        arms = []
        for c in a["cohorts"]:
            s = (c.get("strata") or {}).get("TAF15::NR4A3")
            if not s:
                continue
            if "denom" in s:
                arms.append(s["denom"])
            else:
                arms += [v["denom"] for v in s.values() if isinstance(v, dict) and "denom" in v]
>       pooled = a["analyses"]["B_outcome_by_partner"]["disease_specific_death"]["taf15_arm"]["denom"]

_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 

a = {'_schema': 'emc-fusion-partner-pooling/1', '_generated_by': 'research/manuscripts/emc_fusion_partner_pooling.py', '_g... in its COHORTS table. Change a count in the script and regenerate; never edit a number here (CLAUDE.md rule 1).', ...}

    def _lr_pair(a):
        """Both cohorts' local-recurrence arms, in the order §3.3 states them.
    
        ⚠ ONE CONSTRUCTION, EIGHT GROUPS, ON PURPOSE. The sentence's whole point is that the two cohorts
        run in OPPOSITE directions; binding each rate separately would let a swap that keeps every digit

    def test_the_falsifier_states_the_multiple_of_the_pooled_experience_the_counts_give(flat, art):

        multiple the evidence strictly exceeds is 2 and the prose must read "more than twice". If the
        TAF15 arm grows — the one thing that would really move this — the multiple falls, and a sentence
        still claiming "more than twice" would be overstating how far out of reach the falsifier is,
        which is the direction that flatters this paper.

        construction returns whatever multiple the prose states; asserting the RIGHT word is present
        would pass a sentence that also stated a wrong one somewhere else, and asserting a wrong word is
        absent would pass a sentence that stated none.

_____________ test_every_artifact_pointer_the_prose_names_resolves _____________

prose = '---\nid: DOC-EMC-FUSION-PARTNER-STRATIFICATION\ntitle: "Fusion-variant stratification in EMC (EWSR1::NR4A3 vs TAF15::...rseded, retained: "below the ≈27 % the field routinely quotes from a single referral-centre series" — Appendix A30.*\n'
art = {'_schema': 'emc-fusion-partner-pooling/1', '_generated_by': 'research/manuscripts/emc_fusion_partner_pooling.py', '_g... in its COHORTS table. Change a count in the script and regenerate; never edit a number here (CLAUDE.md rule 1).', ...}

    def test_every_artifact_pointer_the_prose_names_resolves(prose, art):
        """⛔ A POINTER TO A KEY THAT DOES NOT EXIST IS A BROKEN CITATION OF THE EVIDENCE.
    
        Two appendix rows in this document pointed at
        `analyses.B_outcome_by_partner.counter_signal.metastasis_reading`; there is no `counter_signal`
        node, and the real path is `analyses.B_outcome_by_partner.metastasis_reading`. A reader
        following it finds nothing and cannot tell whether the claim or the path is wrong. A blind seat
        found it; the check costs nothing and finds the whole class.
    
        ⚠ THE FOREIGN ROOTS ARE DECLARED, NOT SKIPPED. A path whose first segment is not a top-level key
        of this artifact is either a pointer into a different file — which must be in
        `FOREIGN_POINTERS` — or a typo. Skipping unknown roots silently would let a mistyped root pass
        as "not ours".
        """
        seen, broken, undeclared = 0, [], []
        for m in _POINTER.finditer(prose):
            path = m.group(1)
            if _FILE_SUFFIX.search(path):
                continue
            root = re.match(r"[A-Za-z0-9_]+", path).group(0)
            if root not in art:
                if path not in FOREIGN_POINTERS:
                    undeclared.append(path)
                continue
            seen += 1
            ok, why = _resolve(art, path)
            if not ok:
                broken.append(f"{path} — {why}")
        assert not undeclared, (
            f"the manuscript names path-shaped references {undeclared} whose root is not a top-level "
            "key of the pooling artifact and which are not declared in FOREIGN_POINTERS. Either fix the "
            "path or declare which artifact it points into.")
>       assert not broken, (
            "the manuscript points at artifact keys that do not exist: " + "; ".join(broken))
E       AssertionError: the manuscript points at artifact keys that do not exist: analyses.B_outcome_by_partner.metastasis_reading — 'metastasis_reading' is not a key here; analyses.B_outcome_by_partner.metastasis_reading — 'metastasis_reading' is not a key here; analyses.B_outcome_by_partner.metastasis_reading — 'metastasis_reading' is not a key here; analyses.B_outcome_by_partner.metastasis_reading — 'metastasis_reading' is not a key here
E       assert not ["analyses.B_outcome_by_partner.metastasis_reading — 'metastasis_reading' is not a key here", "analyses.B_outcome_by_p...ading' is not a key here", "analyses.B_outcome_by_partner.metastasis_reading — 'metastasis_reading' is not a key here"]

flat = '--- id: DOC-EMC-FUSION-PARTNER-STRATIFICATION title: "Fusion-variant stratification in EMC (EWSR1::NR4A3 vs TAF15::NR...perseded, retained: "below the ≈27 % the field routinely quotes from a single referral-centre series" — Appendix A30. '

    def test_every_fraction_and_percentage_is_bound_or_declared(flat, art):
        """★★ THE SURFACE ENUMERATED, NOT SAMPLED (paper-hardening §8a).
    
        A guard is only as good as the set of sentences it reaches, and "how many did it reach" is a
        question you answer by counting, not by reading. Every `events/denom` fraction and every
        one-decimal rate in the manuscript is required to fall inside the span of a BINDING or of a
        declared not-artifact-owned construction. A new figure added to the prose with no binding fails
        here rather than joining the document unwatched.
    
        ⚠ THIS IS COVERAGE, NOT CORRECTNESS. It says only that something would notice a change; the
        bindings are what say the value is right.
        """
        spans = _covered_spans(flat, art)
        uncovered = []
        for m in _CENSUS.finditer(flat):
            if not any(s <= m.start() and m.end() <= e for s, e in spans):
                uncovered.append((m.group(0), flat[max(0, m.start() - 90):m.end() + 60]))
>       assert not uncovered, (
            f"{len(uncovered)} printed figure(s) in the manuscript are bound to nothing and declared "

    def test_every_statistical_quantity_is_bound_or_declared(flat, art):
        """★★ THE SECOND CENSUS, over the notations the first one cannot see.
    
        Same contract as `test_every_fraction_and_percentage_is_bound_or_declared` and the same two
        buckets — a BINDING watches the value, a DECLARATION only accounts for it — applied to cohort
        sizes, p-values in both printed forms, hazard ratios, and measurements in centimetres or
        months.
    
        ⚠ THIS IS COVERAGE, NOT CORRECTNESS, and the distinction matters more here than above: several
        of these figures are quoted from sources rather than computed, so what a binding proves is that
        the manuscript still states what the artifact records the source as having published.
        """
        spans = _covered_spans(flat, art)
        uncovered = []
        for m in _CENSUS_STATS.finditer(flat):
            if not any(s <= m.start() and m.end() <= e for s, e in spans):
                uncovered.append((m.group(0), flat[max(0, m.start() - 90):m.end() + 60]))

    def test_the_declared_exclusions_still_describe_real_sentences(flat):
        """⛔ AN EXCLUSION THAT NO LONGER MATCHES ANYTHING IS AN EXCLUSION NOBODY WILL EVER REMOVE.

research/manuscripts/tests/test_fusion_partner_prose_matches_its_artifact.py:2242: AssertionError
________ test_the_guard_binds_a_material_share_of_the_documents_figures ________

flat = '--- id: DOC-EMC-FUSION-PARTNER-STRATIFICATION title: "Fusion-variant stratification in EMC (EWSR1::NR4A3 vs TAF15::NR...perseded, retained: "below the ≈27 % the field routinely quotes from a single referral-centre series" — Appendix A30. '
art = {'_schema': 'emc-fusion-partner-pooling/1', '_generated_by': 'research/manuscripts/emc_fusion_partner_pooling.py', '_g... in its COHORTS table. Change a count in the script and regenerate; never edit a number here (CLAUDE.md rule 1).', ...}

    def test_the_guard_binds_a_material_share_of_the_documents_figures(flat, art):
        """★ A FLOOR UNDER COVERAGE, so that deleting bindings cannot quietly become the way to green.
    
        Not a target to chase — the census above is the real instrument. This is the ratchet that stops
        the file from being emptied: if a binding is removed, either the sentence went with it (and the
        census stays green while this falls) or the coverage really dropped.
        """
        bound, declared = _spans_by_bucket(flat)
        total = len(_CENSUS.findall(flat))
        assert total >= 100, (
            f"only {total} printed figures found; the census pattern has stopped matching the document")
    
        # ⚠ COUNT THE FIGURES A DECLARATION IS THE *ONLY* THING COVERING. Several declared patterns —
        # "95 %" above all — also fall inside bound constructions, so counting every declaration match
        # would report a document far less guarded than it is.
        only_declared = [m.group(0) for m in _CENSUS.finditer(flat)
                         if not any(s <= m.start() and m.end() <= e for s, e in bound)
                         and any(s <= m.start() and m.end() <= e for s, e in declared)]
>       assert len(only_declared) <= 20, (
            f"{len(only_declared)} figures rest on a declaration alone: {sorted(set(only_declared))}. "
            "That list is meant to stay short and reviewable; a growing exclusion set is how a guard "
            "stops measuring anything.")
E       AssertionError: 25 figures rest on a declaration alone: ['1 %', '1186/2045', '27 %', '35/67', '80 %', '90%', '95 %']. That list is meant to stay short and reviewable; a growing exclusion set is how a guard stops measuring anything.
E       assert 25 <= 20
E        +  where 25 = len(['95 %', '95 %', '95 %', '90%', '95 %', '95 %', ...])

flat = '--- id: DOC-EMC-FUSION-PARTNER-STRATIFICATION title: "Fusion-variant stratification in EMC (EWSR1::NR4A3 vs TAF15::NR...perseded, retained: "below the ≈27 % the field routinely quotes from a single referral-centre series" — Appendix A30. '
art = {'_schema': 'emc-fusion-partner-pooling/1', '_generated_by': 'research/manuscripts/emc_fusion_partner_pooling.py', '_g... in its COHORTS table. Change a count in the script and regenerate; never edit a number here (CLAUDE.md rule 1).', ...}

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("what,pattern,count", COUNT_WORDS, ids=[r[0] for r in COUNT_WORDS])
    def test_every_count_the_prose_spells_out_is_the_count_the_artifact_holds(what, pattern, count,
                                                                             flat, art):
        """⛔ EVERY SITE, AND THE COMPARISON IS CASE-FOLDED BECAUSE THE WORD OPENS SENTENCES.
    
        "Two cohorts publish EMC outcome event counts" opens §3.3 and sits mid-sentence in §2.3a's
        quotation of it. Capitalisation is a typographic fact about position; the count is the claim.
    
        ⛔ AND A LOST MATCH IS A FAILURE, NOT A PASS. A pattern that stops matching is a guard that has
        silently stopped guarding — which is how §5's cardinal survived a repair that replaced the
        continuation of its own sentence. When this fires, check the MEANING before the regex: re-anchor
        only if the sentence says the same thing in different words.
        """
        expected = _word(count(art))
        found = re.findall(pattern, flat)
        assert found, (
            f"nothing in this synthesis's prose matches the construction that states {what} "
            f"(/{pattern}/). Either the sentence was reworded — check the MEANING before the regex — or "
            "the claim was dropped.")
        wrong = [f for f in found if f.casefold() != expected.casefold()]
>       assert not wrong, (
            f"{what}: the artifact gives {expected!r} and the prose spells {wrong!r} at {len(wrong)} of "
            f"its {len(found)} site(s). The count is derived from the cohort records, not typed — fix "
            "the prose, or fix the generator and regenerate. Never edit the predicate to match the "
            "page.")
E       AssertionError: the number of series that ran a multivariable model: the artifact gives 'zero' and the prose spells ['one', 'one'] at 2 of its 2 site(s). The count is derived from the cohort records, not typed — fix the prose, or fix the generator and regenerate. Never edit the predicate to match the page.
E       assert not ['one', 'one']
